# Evidence Ledger

Per Plan ground rule 3 (V2-COMPLETION-PLAN), every completed task appends a row here:
task ID, commit SHA, author agent+tier, verifier agent+tier, evidence path:line, a
test-output hash, and a status. Status is one of `implemented — unverified` (the
author's own claim, not yet independently checked), `verified` (an independent
verifier reproduced the acceptance criteria), or `blocked(external)`. A task ID is
never "done" on the strength of its author's claim alone — see
`src/maezo/platform/validation/cli.py:104,121` for the always-pass-unconditionally
anti-pattern this ledger exists to make structurally impossible to repeat.

CI (`.github/workflows/evidence-ledger.yml` / `scripts/ci/check_evidence_ledger.py`)
enforces that any PR whose branch name or body cites a task ID carries a row for it
here at HEAD; the row is added by this table, not by the check.
A PR body only "cites" a task ID via an explicit marker — a `Task:`/`Tasks:` line or a
`[T<phase>.<n>]` bracket — never via a bare prose mention (T0.5 gate-precision follow-up).

Test hash convention: `sha256:` of the sorted `PASSED`/`FAILED` result lines from
`pytest <test file> -v --tb=no -p no:cacheprovider` — reproducible by anyone who
checks out the cited commit and re-runs the same test file, and stable across
machines since it excludes timing/header noise.

| t1.1 | 2026-07-17 | runtime-spine-engineer (R2, defect fix) | adversarial-verifier (R2) | 35b8e3e9b28fc081dba02e4ef55d7321b4dc30b8 | ROOT-CAUSE FIX for a defect independently confirmed against the donor repo (Maezo-Healthcare-Plan carries the identical bug in its v1 harness.py:215, plus fraude.py comments documenting workarounds built specifically around it — donor's own compensation NOT ported into this repo). src/maezo/tools/workers/harness.py: `CibSevenWorkerTransport.fetch_and_lock` extracted inbound variables via bare `.get("value")`, never decoding `Json`-typed entries — CIB Seven's fetchAndLock REST response returns a `Json` variable's `value` as a JSON STRING (mirrors what `_to_camunda_var` already WRITES on complete/bpmnError), so list/dict process variables (e.g. SP-OP-CONTAS-001's `linhas_conta_refs`) arrived at every consuming worker as a raw string, not a Python list/dict; slipped past prior T1.1 verification because those tests used primitive-typed variables only. Fix: new `_from_camunda_var` (symmetric read-side counterpart to `_to_camunda_var`) decodes `type=="Json"` entries via `json.loads` inside `fetch_and_lock`; malformed JSON in a `Json`-typed variable is fail-closed PER-TASK — `handle_failure(retries=0)` reported directly (mirrors the harness's own ValueError/bad-input convention, module docstring §9) and the task excluded from the returned batch, never handed to a worker undecoded and never allowed to abort the rest of the polled batch (proven: one malformed task among two in a single fetchAndLock response does not drop the well-formed one). SYMMETRIC-PATH AUDIT: outbound `_to_camunda_var`'s existing `dict\|list -> Json` branch (json.dumps) was ALREADY correct — proven, not changed, via 2 new round-trip tests (`_to_camunda_var(x)["value"]` fed back through `json.loads` reproduces `x` exactly for both dict and list). FAKE-TRANSPORT AUDIT: `FakeWorkerTransport` never round-trips the wire format at all (tests construct `ExternalTask.variables` directly as Python objects) — already semantically consistent post-fix, proven via a new test rather than changed. COMPENSATION AUDIT: `grep -rn "json.loads" src/maezo/tools/workers/` — 0 hits; no worker module anywhere under this package manually decodes a Json-typed variable, so nothing needed removal (donor's `fraude.py::_as_record_hashes` CSV-workaround-for-this-exact-bug was never ported into this repo's `fraude.py`, which instead silently resets non-list `evidencia_refs` to `[]` today — a separate, disclosed, out-of-scope data-loss smell in code this task's charter forbids touching). RELATED-BUT-OUT-OF-SCOPE finding: `src/maezo/tools/mcp_cibseven/transport.py:272` (`CibSevenHttpTransport.get_process_status`) has the IDENTICAL undecoded-`.get("value")` pattern for a different transport (the agent/graph-facing MCP CIB Seven client, not this worker harness) — untouched, flagged for a follow-up task. | t1.5 | 2026-07-19 | fraude-dmn-input-typing (R1) | — | 838ff09562ddad93a6ecf6c7c19c4ca71334efab (branch t1.5-dmn-input-hardening, atop main 3b62057 incl. #108) | TASK t1.5-dmn-input-hardening — DMN-input pass-through hardening (post-#94, post-#102/#107). FIX 1 (src/maezo/tools/workers/fraude.py): the 5 numeric scoring signals were raw `variables.get()` pass-throughs into the 7 `fraude_scoring/*` DMN tables with NO coercion. NEW `_coerce_numeric(value, kind)` (fraude.py:189-227) coerces a valid int/float/clean-numeric-string to the DMN-declared typeRef (verified per `.dmn`: risk_score/code_tier/provider_volume `integer`, z_score/deviation_pct `double`) and returns `(False, None)` — caller OMITS the key — on ANY non-coercible value (bool [int-subclass, never a numeric signal], non-numeric/empty string, NaN/inf, list/dict, or a FRACTIONAL value for an integer input, e.g. `"80.5"` never `int()->80` which could flip a threshold rule). `_collect_scoring_inputs` (fraude.py:255-289) now routes numeric keys through it (coerce-or-DROP). FAIL-CLOSED: no wrong-typed value ever reaches the DMN; an omitted signal falls to that table's own missing-input catch-all row (conservative). Integer inputs typed WITHOUT a float round-trip (no precision loss past 2**53). Booleans (`_is_true`) unchanged. FIX 2 (semantically dangerous — DROP, not str()-cast): `tuss_codes` REMOVED from `_SCORING_INPUT_KEYS` (fraude.py:157-176 comment). It is a list-per-convention field bound to `unbundling_partial_bundles.dmn` `ie_tuss_codes typeRef="string"` whose column is `-` (wildcard) in EVERY rule (verified — no rule reads it). A blanket `str(["30101012"]) -> "['30101012']"` (repr) would SILENTLY FLIP any future FEEL `starts with(...)`/`contains(...)` rule — strictly worse than today. DROP chosen (recon's cleanest): evaluation byte-identical today (wildcard ignores a missing input), latent footgun removed; the documented alternative (fix DMN typeRef to a collection + deliberate join) is deferred to whenever a rule actually needs it — never a blanket cast. FIX 3 (dmn_transport.py:191-234 docstring, DOC-ONLY, #102 logic untouched): corrected `_to_camunda_vars`'s overstated claim ("no DMN input is currently a bare list/dict … verified against live call sites") to the truth — it types by Python RUNTIME type ONLY, does NOT validate against the DMN's declared typeRef; a wrong-shape source value (list for a string input, LLM-JSON string for an integer input, float for an integer input) is silently typed by its Python type; input-shape correctness is the CALLER's responsibility. FIX 4 (src/maezo/agents/helena/graph.py:383, the T2.7-row-flagged nit): `_coerce_age` `str.isdigit()` -> `str.isdecimal()`. `isdigit()` is True for Unicode digit glyphs `int()` cannot parse (superscript `"²"`, circled digits) so `isdigit()`+`int()` would RAISE at the unwrapped call site (graph.py:796); `isdecimal()` admits only int()-parseable base-10 digits -> fails CLOSED to `(False, None)`. #94 fraude strict-xfails: NONE affected (unit-verified) — their premise is `_FRAUDE_WORKER_KAFKA_GAP_REASON`/`_FRAUDE_NOTIFY_SLA_UNREGISTERED_REASON` (score_indicators never calls kafka.publish), orthogonal to input typing; also the harness decodes process variables via `_from_camunda_var` (harness.py:497-498) BEFORE the worker runs, so the integration test's engine-shaped `z_score={"value":2.5,...}` seed arrives at score_indicators as plain `2.5` and coerces cleanly. Tests: tests/unit/tools/workers/test_fraude.py (+7: coerce-valid per field, fail-closed-drop non-numeric/bool/NaN/list/fractional-for-int, int precision >2**53, tuss_codes never forwarded nor repr-cast, end-to-end omit through score_indicators/fake.calls) + tests/unit/agents/test_helena.py (+1: Unicode-digit glyph fails closed, never raises; demonstrates `"²".isdigit() and not "²".isdecimal()` + `int("²")` raises). Scope held: no pick_fields/base.py touched. | sha256:d29a9d1d0c8e0e030e7913626e710b2fefb69d61e43a28c9e9a967d110c8bc09 (sorted PASSED lines, `pytest tests/unit/tools/workers/test_fraude.py tests/unit/agents/test_helena.py tests/unit/tools/workers/test_dmn_transport.py -v --tb=no -p no:cacheprovider`, 120 passed); full unit lane `pytest tests/unit -q` 2091 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed; `ruff check src tests` + `ruff format --check src tests` clean (301 files); `mypy --strict` on the 3 changed src files — Success, 0 issues; `make check-bpmn-error-allowlist` PASS (12 pre-existing non-blocking dead-model warns, ADR-0030 F5); `make validate-artifacts` OK 0 errors/0 notices. | implemented — pending adversarial verification |
